PROVISIONAL: f98 cube, n_H = 1653 A'; final cube pending
P_th = 1.1 n_H k T (physical)
Alpha paper -- headline numbers table (Shelest+26 conventions)
STATS_BOX: |x|,|y| <= 500 pc, |z| <= 400 pc. Slabs: |z-z_c| <= 30 pc. Percentiles: 15/85.
phase_scheme column: dPdn (headline) | temperature; n/a where the quantity does not depend on the scheme.
MASKED variant: not implemented -- no MASKED definition exists in any reference script.
self_gravity column: off | mean (footprint_mean, DEFAULT); column (per_column) not run in this sweep -- code retained.
cell_selection column (RAW only has both): exclude_him_flag = statistics over non-flagged
  cells -- the default, and every pre-Step-1f number; all_cells = flagged cells included.
  HIM_A/HIM_B keep exclude_him_flag only: they SUBSTITUTE the flagged cells' density, so an all_cells
  statistic would mix model values into the observations. n/a where the quantity does not
  depend on a cell selection (Sigma_gas, the phase fractions).
Thermal pressure: p_nT = n_H T is used ONLY for classification (the HIM flag
  and the phase scheme) and is never reported; every number below uses
  p_th_phys = 1.1 n_H T.
sigma_eff = sqrt(alpha)*c_s (TOTAL, = sqrt(P_tot/rho)); sigma_nt = sqrt(3(alpha-1))*c_s
  (NON-THERMAL -- this is what Step 1c reported under the name "sigma_eff").
alpha has THREE estimators, all over the same cells and weights:
  alpha_median_of_ratios = weighted median of per-cell P_tot,i/P_th,i
  alpha_mean_of_ratios   = weighted mean   of per-cell P_tot,i/P_th,i
  alpha_ratio_of_means   = sum(w P_tot)/sum(w P_th)  (NEW; conserves pressure)
  alpha_p15/p85_of_ratios = percentiles OF THE RATIOS.
Mach_from_* and sigma_nt_from_* are derived from each estimator separately and
  are NaN where that alpha < 1; frac_alpha_lt1 reports how many cells sit there.
Vertical profiles (summary.npz only) are binned at 4 pc; the slabs are
  +-30 pc. Binning is statistics only -- P_tot, Sigma_gas and the
  self-gravity integrals use the real 2 pc grid.
  -- see results/README.md for full column definitions.
